6a1(1): @#//bsags shing /tshogs rdzogs par byas pa'i rgyud can /sgyu dang /g.yo dang /rdzun dang /phra ma dang /ngag rtsu bo dang /gsod pa dang /gnod par byed 
6a2(1): pa dang /'chang ba dang /lta ba dan pas gzings pa dang /sdigs pa dang /rtsod pa dang /'thab pa dang /'breg pa dang /khrug lod dang /nye ba'i nyon mongs pa dang bral ba sha stag dang //
6a3(1): 'jig rten skyong ba bzhi 'di lta ste /rgyal po chen po rnam thos kyi bu dang /rgyal po chen po yul 'khor srung dang /rgyal po chen po 'phags skyes po dang /rgyal po chen po mig gi bzang rang rang 
6a4(1): g.yog 'khor gyis bskor ba rnams dang /gnod sbyin gyi sde dpon chen po lngas rtsen rang gi bu'i 'khor dang bcas pa dang /gnod sbyin chen po lag na rdo rje rang bzhin gyi 'khor dang bcas pa dang/
6a5(1): 'byung po chen po'i mi 'phro ma rang gnod sbyin mo'i 'khor dang bcas pa dang /dud can ma dang /mdung thogs ma dang /ser mo dang /dka' zlog ma dang /dpal gyi lha mo chen po dang /
6a6(1): dbyangs can ma dang /'jigs byed pa ma dang /zla ba dang /lha'i dbang po brgya byin dang /lha'i bu dbang phyug chen po dang /mi mjed kyi bdag po tshangs ba rang gi lha'i bu'i 'khor dang bcas
6a7(1): pa dag dang /klu'i rgyal po ma 'dros pa dang /klu'i rgyal po rgya mtsho rang rang gi klu'i 'khor dang bcas pa dag dang /nam mkha' lding gi dbang po gzi chen dang /nam mkha' lding ga 
6b1(1): dbang po lus chen rang rang gi nam mkha' lding gi 'khor dang bcas pa dag dang /lham yin gyi dbang po stobs can dang /lha ma yin gyi dbang po sgrag can rang rang gi tshogs kyi 'khor dang 
6b2(1): bcas pa dag dang /mi 'am ci'i (byang po )ljon pa rang gi mi 'am ci'i 'khor dang bcas pa dang /de las gzhan pa lha dang /klu dang /gnod sbyin dang /dri za dang /lha ma yin dang /nam mkha' lding 
6b3(1): dang /mi 'am ci dang /lto 'phye chen po dang /mi dang mi ma yin pa gzi brjid che ba /gzi brjid che ba dpag tu med pa dag dang /grangs med pa dag dang /thabs cig ste /rigs 
6b4(1): bzhi po bram zer dang /rgyal rigs dang /rje rigs dang /dmang rigs rnams dang /'khor bzhi po dge slong dang /dge slong ma dang /dge bsnyen ma rnams dang /shAkya bye ba khrag 
6b5(1): khrig 'bum dag dang /rgyal po rnams dang /blon po chen po rnams dang /grong brdal gyi mi rnams dang /yul (gyi )mi rnams dang /khyim bdag dang /blon po dang /pho brang 'khor gyi mi 
6b6(1): dang /sgo dpon gyi 'khor rnams kyis bskor cing mdun gyis ltas nas/bkur sti byas /bla mar byas /ri mor byas /mchod pa byas te /bcom ldan 'das kyis na bza' 
6b7(1): dang /zhal zas dang /gzims cha dang /gdan dang /bsnyun gsos sman dang /yo byad mang po gya nom pa dag bsnyes shing phyogs bcu'i 'jig rten gyi khams rab 'byam 
7a1(1): @#/ O /dpag tu med /grangs pa dag tu yang bcom ldan 'das kyi grags pa dang /sgra dang tshigs su bcad pa rgya cher mngon par grags so //rdo rje snying po 'i sa phyogs 
7a2(1): mnyam zhing yid du 'ong la /rgya che zhing yangs pa /chag chag legs par btab pa /phyag dar legs par byas bya pa /legs par rnam par phye ba /phog snod nas bdugs pa /me tog 
7a3(1): ut+ba la dang /pad+ma dang /ku mu ta dang /pad+ma dkar po dang /a ti mu g+ta ka dang /ka dam+ba dang /dar+ci ka dang /dar ni dang sgya snar dang /mya ngan 'tshang dang /ko ran+da ka dang /sna ma dang /yu

7a5(1): ba /rin po che'i bla bre bres pa /rin po che'i dril bu g.yer ka'i dra ba bres pa /rin po che'i lnga ldis sbyangs pa /rin po che sna tshogasakyi smras pa /nor bu ga dang /gser dang /rdo rje dang /bai DUr+ya dang /mu gtig dang /dung dang /
7a6(1): man shel dang /byu ru dang /an+da rnyil dang /rdo'i snying po dang /ma rgad dang /sbug dang /shel dang /rin po che bye ba khrag khrig 'bum dag gis rgyun pa la bzhugs te /dbang po zhi ba /thugs zhi ba /dul ba/zhi gnas dam 
7a7(1): pa brnyes pa /dul ba dang /zhi gnas mchog brnyes pa /bsrungs pa /gtso bor gyur pa /dbang po thul ba /mtsho ltar dung zhing /gsal la rnyog ba /med pa /rin po che'i mchod stong ltar mngon par 'phags pa //
7b1(1): dpal 'bar zhing /lham me lha nor bzhugs ba /sku skyes bu chen po'i mchan sum cu rtsa gnyis kyi legs par brgyan pa /sku dpe byad bzang pos mdzes par byas pa/rgya mtsho chen po ltar dam pa'i chos kyi 
7b2(1): chus yongs su gang ba/ri rab ltar mi bskyod pa /sa ltar sems can thams cad kyi nye bar 'tsho ba chu ltar dge ba'i rtsa ba rab tu bskyed ba/tha snyad ltar thugs mnyam pa /nam mkha' ltar gos med 
7b3(1): med pa/nyi ma ltar mi shes pa'i mun pa sel ba /zla ba ltar dkar po'i chos yongs su rdzogs pa/yid bzhin gyi nor bu ltar bsam pa thams cad yongs su rdzogs par mdzad pa/nyi ma ltar gzi 
7b4(1): rjid gyis lam me ba /zla ba ltar gsil zhing sim par mdzad pa /rgya mtsho ltar rin po che 'i 'byung gnas su gyur pa /rgya mtsho ltar za ba pa/me'i phung po ltar snang ba/lus mi skyod pa /sems mi g.yo 
7b5(1): ba|zhi zhing rab tu zhi ba /dbang po 'khrul ba mi mnga' ba /ma khengs zhing mi rgod pa /dran pa legs bar nye bar bzhag pa spyod lam mdzes ba/legs par mnyam bar bzhag pa/rtag tu mnyam 
7b6(1): par bzhag pa /phyi ma'i mtha'i bskal ba bye ba phrag 'bum du dge ba'i rtsa ba thams cad yang dag par bsgrubs pa/bsod nams kyi tshogs bsogs pa/pha rol tu phyin pa thams cad la 
7b7(1): thabs mkhas pa /rtogs par thugs su chud pa /sangs rgyas dang byang chub sems dpa' sa thams cad la rnam par rol ba /byang chub sems dpa'i spyod pa thams cad thugs kyis spyod pa /
8a1(1): @#//sems can thams cad kyis phan pa dang /bde ba 'thob bar bya ba'i phyir brtson pa de chos ston te /tshangs par spyod pa /thog mar dge ba /bar du dge ba /tha mar dge ba /
8a2(1): don bzang po ma 'dres pa yongs su rdzogs pa /yongs su dag pa /yongs su byang ba /dri ma med pa /'od gsal ba /yid du 'ong ba /yid du thad pa /yid sim bar byed pa /yid shin du dga' bar byed pa /
8a3(1): yid skur bar byed pa /yid mgu zhing rgyas par byed pa /rnam par dag pa /'jigs pa med pa /mi rtsub pa /brtan pa /zab pa mi g.yo ba /mi 'gyur ba /brtag med pa /rtog ge'i spyod yul ma yin pa /
8a4(1): bsam gyis mi khyab pa /rmad du byung ba /brjod du med pa'i yang brjod du med pa ston cing 'chad de rnam 'byed pa /'brel zhing gsal bar mdzad do //de'i tshe byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen 
8a5(1): po ri'i phug dang /zom dang /ri sul dang /gseb dang /sman ljongs na seng ge'i mchog rnam par bskyings shing nga ro rnam par sgrogs pa lta bu'i gtsug gi nor bu zhes bya ba /gzugs bzangs ba //
8a6(1): mdzes pa /lta na sdug pa /kha dog bzang po rgyas pa mchog dang /ldan pa /sngon gyi rgyal ba rnams la lhag par bya ba byas pa /dge ba'i rtsa ba bskyed pa /sangs rgyas bye ba khrag khrig 'bum mang po

8b1(1): snying rtse ba can /snying rje dang ldan ba/nges pa grogs ba bde ba/mkhas pa mdzangs pa /gsal ba /yid gzhungs pa shes gnyen dang ldan pa /le lo med pa /shin tu dul ba /dge nges che ba /sgyu can ma
8b2(1): yin pa /g.yo med pa /mi brtsub pa /mi brlang ba /drang ba /nga rgyal dang /rgyags pa dang /dregs pa dang /'thab pa dang /khro ba dang /phra dog dang /ser sna dang /ngar 'dzin pa dang /nga yir 'dzin pa 
8b3(1): lhag par zhen pa dang /sdug bsngal ba dang /yid mi bde ba dang /ma rig pa dang bral ba dang /bsod nams dang ldan pa zhig 'khor de nyid du 'dus par gyur te 'jug go/de nas byang chub sems 
8b4(1): dpa' sems dpa' chen po ri'i phug dang /zom dang /ri'i sul dang /gseb dang /sman ljoms na seng ge mchog rnam par bsgyings zhing nga ro rnam par sgrogs pa lta bu'i mi'i dbang po'i gtsug gi 
8b5(1): nor bu stan las langs te /bla gos phrag pa gcig gis tu zal nas /pus mo g.yas pa'i lha nga ma ma pad+ma'i snying po la btsugs te /bcom ldan 'das ga la ba dor logs thal mo sbyar 
8b6(1): te btung nas /bcom ldan 'das la 'di skad ces gsol to /gal te bdag gis zhus nas zhus ba lan gdab pa'i slad du /bcom ldan 'das kyis skabs phye nas de bzhin gshegs pa 
8b7(1): dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas la bdag phyogs 'ga' zhig zhu bar 'tshal lo /de ces gsol ba dang /bcom ldan 'das kyisabyang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' 
9a1(1): @#/ O /chen po ri'i phug dang /zom dang /ri sul dang /gseb dang /sman ljongs nas /seng ge'i mchog rnam par bsgyings shing nga ro rnam par sgrogs lta bu'i mi dbang po gtsug gi 
9a2(1): nor bu la 'di skad ces bka' stsal to //rigs kyi bu khyod la sangs rgyas dang /byang chub sems dpa' thams cad rtag du go skabs 'byed kyis /kha rigs kyi bu khyod kyis de bzhin gshegs

9b5(1): dpa' sems dpa' chen poni srog bcod pa spangs pa yin /log par lta ba'i bar yang dag spangs pa yin no //rigs kyi bu de la byang chub sems dpa' semams dpa' chen po srog bcod pa spangs paji lta 
9b6(1): yin zhe na /rigs kyi bu 'di byang chub sems dpa' so de 'di snyam du sems /te /srog ces bya ba ni tha na srog chags grog spur yan chad la yang sdug cing 'phags la dga' zhing yid du 'ong ba yin 
9b7(1): te /bdag la srog 'di sdug cing 'phags la dag zhing yid du 'ong ba yinabagyis su yang dag la gsod pa 'am /gsod du 'jug pa 'am /bsod gyis kyang su la yang bsad par mi bya 
10a1(1): @#/ O /bsod du gzhug par mi bya /gsod pa la rjes su yi rang mi bya'o //rigs kyi bu de ltar na byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po srog bcod pa spangs pa
10a2(1): yin no //de la 'di skad ces bya ste/mnga' na gnas pa yan chad kyang /kun la srog ni sdug pa'i phyir /bdag gi srog 'phangs gang yin pa /de ni srog chags gsod mi byed /de la ma byin
10a3(1): par len par spangs pa ci lta bu yin zhe na /rigs kyi bu 'di la byang chub sems dpa' de 'di ji snyam du sems te /nor zhes bya ba ni tha ma rigs nang ba dang /g.yung po yan 
10a4(1): chad la yang sdug cing 'phangs la dga' zhing yid du 'ong yin te /bdag la nor 'di sdug cing 'phags la dga' zhing yid du 'od ba yin bgyis su yang bdag la 'phrog pa'am/'phrog 
10a5(1): du 'jug ba dang /'phrog pa la rjes su yi rang bar ma gyur cig /bdag gis kyang su la yang 'gro par mi bya'o //'phrog du gzhug par mi bya /'phrog pa'i rjes su yi rang mi bya'o //snyam mo //rigs kyi bu de ltar

10a7(1): nor sdug pa//de ni nor la 'phrog mi byed /de la 'dod la log par g.yem pa spangs pa ji lta bu yin zhe na /rigs kyi bu 'di la byang chub sems dpa' de 'di snyam du sems te /chung ma zhes

14a1(1): @#/ O /pa zhes bya ba de ni nyon mongs pa sred pa thams cad sel bar 'gyur ba'i tshigs bla dgas yin te /de lta bas na bdag gis skom gyis spyin par bya /gor ma bcag snyano//
14a2(1): de sgom gyi spyin pa spyin pa na /sangs rgyas kyi sngags pa'i smon lam bzhin du skom gyi spyin pa 'dis ni /bdag dang sems can thams cad kyi nyon mongs pa sred pa'i rgya mtsho thams cad skems 
14a3(1): par byed par gyur cig/rnam par grol ba'i ro'i skom gyis kyang tshim par byed par gyur cig ces smon lam 'debs /'di la tshig phyi ma yang yod de /rga shi chu srin gyis krugs pa'i /nyon mongs
14a4(1): sred pa'i mtsho sgams nas/rnam grol ro yi sgoms gyis ni /mi rnams bdag gis tshim par bya /sgom byin pas ni mkhas pa de /kun du 'bar zhing rab 'bar ba'i /yi dgas nyam

14a6(1): gzhon pa'i spyin pa zhes bya ba de ni bde ba dang rdzu 'phrul gyi bzhi thams cad sdudpa'i tshig bla dag yin gyis/bdag gi gzhon pa'i spyin pa byin par bya ste /bzhon pa shing rta 'am /gzhon pa

14b1(1): bzhin du gzhon pa'i sbyin pa 'dis na bdag sems can thams cad kyi bde ba dang /rdzu 'phrul gyi gzhi thams cad sdud par gyur gcig ces smon lam 'debs so //de la 'di skad ces bya ste /bzhon 
14b2(1): pa shing rta byin pa 'ayis /rtag du sems cad thams cad kyis /theg pa chen po thob gyur cig/rdzu 'phrul rkang pa thob par shog/bzhon pa'i sbyin pa'i drang (dang) che /sangs rgyas zhing dag tu //
14b3(1): blo ldan bya yi shugs 'dra bar /rdzu 'phrul gyi(sa) ni mkha' las 'gro /de la gos sbyin pa la ji ltar mngon par brtson par byed ce na /rigs kyi bu 'di la byang chub sems dpa' 'di snyam
14b4(1): du sems te /gos sbyin pa zhes bya ba de ni ngo tsha shes pa dang /khrel yod pa dang /mdog yongs su dag par byed pa'i tshig bla dgas yin gyis /bdag gis gos kyi sbyin pa sbyin par
